matched substring.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def _flatten(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        s = m.group(0)\n        s = re.sub(r\"<[^>]+>\", \"\", s)  # drop tags inside placeholder\n        s = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \"\", s)  # placeholders should not contain whitespace\n        return s\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_SPANNING_TAGS_RE.sub(_flatten, xml_text)\n\n\ndef build_replacements(overview: dict, actions: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, str], str, str, str]:\n    summary = overview.get(\"summary\") or {}\n\n    required_summary_keys = [\n        \"점검ID\",\n        \"현장명\",\n        \"주관점검자\",\n \"\"\"\n\n    def _repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key not in mapping:\n            raise KeyError(f\"No replacement provided for placeholder: {key}\")\n        return inspection date.\n    return xml_text.replace(dash_date, dot_date)\n\n\ndef append_risk_severity_note(xml_text: str, risk_tier: str) -> str:\n    notes = {\n        \"High\": \"즉시조치\",\n        \"Medium\": \"계획보완\",\n        \"Low\": \"모니터링\",\n    }\n    if risk_tier not in notes:\n        # If tier is unexpected, still leave it alone rather than guessing.\n        return xml_text\n\n    note = notes[risk_tier]\n\n    # Replace tier occurrences not already annotated.\n    # Avoid turning \"High 즉시조치\" into \"High 즉시조치 즉시조치\".\n    pat =
